## This Week in Security: Your News Briefing

Welcome to your weekly security roundup. We've tracked down the **10 most important stories** this
week—the ones everyone's talking about, from critical threats to emerging trends that could shape
your security posture. Leading the news this week is **CVE-2026-85046**, which has sparked
conversation across 6 sources. Meanwhile, the industry is closely tracking **CVE-2026-80130 | Dell
Secure Connect Gateway path traversal** with 4 mentions, along with emerging details on
**CVE-2026-77697 | Zohocorp ManageEngine Endpoint Central...**, **Threat Modeling and Social
Issues**, and **Hackers are using 'invisible' Unicode characters to sneak phishing...**. Here's the
full breakdown of what you need to know.

### 🚨 Critical Threats This Week

First, the stories that demand your immediate attention:

**1. CVE-2026-85046**
   Mentioned across 6 industry sources this week. Currently trending CVE - Hype Score: 11 - Type confusion in V8 in Google Chrome prior to 152.
   [Get the details →](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-85046)

**2. CVE-2026-80130 | Dell Secure Connect Gateway path traversal**
   Mentioned across 4 industry sources this week. A vulnerability marked as very critical has been reported in Dell Secure Connect Gateway.
   [Get the details →](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-80130)

**3. CVE-2026-77697 | Zohocorp ManageEngine Endpoint Central 11.4.2508.13/11.4.2528.34 privileges management (EUVD-2026-72340)**
   Mentioned across 4 industry sources this week. A vulnerability was found in Zohocorp ManageEngine Endpoint Central 11.
   [Get the details →](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-77697)

### 🛠️ Tools, Updates & Releases

New capabilities and releases worth knowing about:

**1. Sam Altman calls GPT-6 Astra rollout ‘messy’ as enterprise users wait for access**
   OpenAI’s rollout of its GPT-6 Astra model ran into early access issues after paying ChatGPT users we. Referenced in 2 stories this week.
   [Explore →](https://www.computerworld.com/article/4219264/sam-altman-calls-gpt-6-astra-rollout-messy-as-enterprise-users-wait-for-access-2.html)

**2. Promoting AI Security — Acting Assistant Privacy Commissioner Speaks at the GenA.I. Symposium**
   . Referenced in 2 stories this week.
   [Explore →](http://www.pcpd.org.hk/english/whatsnew/20251031.html)

**3. ASCII smuggling isn't just an AI security risk**
   Phishers find a new use for invisible Unicode tag characters. Referenced in 3 stories this week.
   [Explore →](https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/09/04/ascii-smuggling-isnt-just-an-ai-security-risk/5294595)

### What You Should Do Next

**Monitor these** in your environment next week:
- Any new CVE announcements related to systems you operate
- Emerging attack techniques being discussed in the community
- Updates and patches for tools your team uses

**Have a look at** the full deep-dives in the trending stories below. Each one provides context that could inform your security decisions this week.
